Story So Far

A readable summary of what happened in the campaign before the technical diagnostics.

Across 100 turns, the campaign followed an investigation that began inside the Rusty Flagon Tavern and gradually widened toward trouble on the road.

Bran admits that a cloaked traveler left the tavern in a hurry and may have seen something important. The player follows the lead and finds the cloaked traveler, resolving the immediate witness search. The player finds the witness trail at the side door and street. The player follows the road signs beyond the tavern, pushing the campaign toward the bandit threat.

Active unresolved objectives: Find the witness, Report findings to Bran.

Lore, Setting, and Director Setup

The premise, stakes, and setting context that frame the campaign run.

The player begins in the Rusty Flagon Tavern after rumors of a witness, a hurried traveler, and possible bandit activity begin to converge. Bran is worried because trouble on the road threatens his patrons, his tavern, and the fragile trust of travelers passing through. The director's dramatic question is: Can the player turn tavern rumors into a reliable lead before the trail goes cold? Setting details: The Rusty Flagon Tavern: A busy roadside tavern where rumors, paid information, and trouble often cross paths. The Bandit Road: A road outside town associated with disappearances, ambushes, and travelers who prefer silence.

The Rusty Flagon Tavern

A busy roadside tavern where rumors, paid information, and trouble often cross paths.

The Bandit Road

A road outside town associated with disappearances, ambushes, and travelers who prefer silence.

Location Journey

Where the run traveled, what happened there, who was involved, and what objectives were tied to each place.

The Rusty Flagon Tavern

Turns: 1–100 · Turn Count: 85

The social hub where the witness investigation begins. Bran lowers his voice and glances toward the tavern door as your question lands. Bran reacts to the question, and the surrounding noise seems to thin as the conversation draws attention. Following the lead brings the witness thread into focus; the immediate search is resolved.

NPCs: Bran, Cloaked Traveler, Local Patron, NPC

Objectives: Find the witness, Report findings to Bran

Location events
[
  "Bran reveals the first witness lead",
  "The witness is found",
  "The witness trail is found",
  "The investigation turns toward the bandit trail"
]

The Bandit Road

Turns: 6–84 · Turn Count: 15

The external danger path revealed after the witness report. Bran reacts to the question, and the surrounding noise seems to thin as the conversation draws attention. The scene shifts with the movement, carrying the pressure of the current lead into the space ahead. The search draws out grounded details from the scene: small marks, watchful faces, and signs of recent tension.

NPCs: Bran, Local Patron

Objectives: None captured
